We introduce Evidotes, an information support system that augments individual posts with relevant scientific and anecdotal information retrieved using three user-selectable lenses (dive deeper, focus on positivity, and big picture). In a mixed-methods study with 17 chronic illness patients, Evidotes improved self-reported information satisfaction (3.2->4.6) and reduced self-reported emotional cost (3.4->1.9) compared to participants' baseline browsing. Moreover, by co-presenting sources, Evidotes unlocked information symbiosis: anecdotes made research accessible and contextual, while research helped filter and generalize peer stories. Our work enables an effective integration of scientific evidence and human anecdotes to help users better manage health uncertainty.

</details>


### [228] [Say It My Way: Exploring Control in Conversational Visual Question Answering with Blind Users](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16930)
*Farnaz Zamiri Zeraati,Yang Trista Cao,Yuehan Qiao,Hal Daumé,Hernisa Kacorri*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 盲人用户通过提示工程定制视觉问答系统交互，研究发现现有系统存在冗长、空间距离估计不准、图像框架不可访问等问题，用户通过自定义提示技术绕过限制


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用生成式AI已有成熟的提示和引导技术，但面向盲人的辅助视觉问答工具仍采用僵化的交互模式，缺乏定制机会。当系统响应与用户目标和情境不匹配时，用户控制尤为重要，这对依赖系统获取视觉信息的盲人用户尤为关键。

Method: 邀请11名盲人用户定制真实世界对话式VQA系统的交互，基于418次交互、反思和后续访谈，分析参与者采用的提示技术，包括研究中引入的和在真实环境中独立开发的。

Result: VQA交互通常较长：参与者平均3轮对话，有时达21轮，输入文本通常比听到的响应短十倍。基于最先进LLM的系统缺乏冗长控制，空间和时间距离估计有限，依赖不可访问的图像框架，几乎没有相机指导。研究发现提示工程等定制技术能帮助参与者绕过这些限制。

Conclusion: 提供了新的公开数据集，并为查询和系统层面的交互设计提供见解。提示工程等定制技术能增强盲人用户对VQA系统的控制，但需要改进系统设计以更好地支持可访问性需求。

Abstract: Prompting and steering techniques are well established in general-purpose generative AI, yet assistive visual question answering (VQA) tools for blind users still follow rigid interaction patterns with limited opportunities for customization. User control can be helpful when system responses are misaligned with their goals and contexts, a gap that becomes especially consequential for blind users that may rely on these systems for access. We invite 11 blind users to customize their interactions with a real-world conversational VQA system. Drawing on 418 interactions, reflections, and post-study interviews, we analyze prompting-based techniques participants adopted, including those introduced in the study and those developed independently in real-world settings. VQA interactions were often lengthy: participants averaged 3 turns, sometimes up to 21, with input text typically tenfold shorter than the responses they heard. Built on state-of-the-art LLMs, the system lacked verbosity controls, was limited in estimating distance in space and time, relied on inaccessible image framing, and offered little to no camera guidance. We discuss how customization techniques such as prompt engineering can help participants work around these limitations. Alongside a new publicly available dataset, we offer insights for interaction design at both query and system levels.

</details>


### [229] [Nudging Attention to Workplace Meeting Goals: A Large-Scale, Preregistered Field Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16939)
*Lev Tankelevitch,Ava Elizabeth Scott,Nagaravind Challakere,Payod Panda,Sean Rintel*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 在微软进行的预注册现场实验中，研究人员测试了轻量级目标反思干预对会议效果的影响，发现干预本身对会议效果无显著影响，但后会议调查意外成为干预手段，提高了目标意识和行为


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无效会议普遍存在，提前明确思考会议目标可能提高会议效果，但当前协作平台缺乏集成支持，需要研究如何通过技术干预改善会议意向性

Method: 在全球科技公司进行的预注册现场实验，361名员工参与7196次会议；实验组在协作平台完成简短会前调查，促使关注即将召开的会议目标；两组均完成后会议调查评估会议效果；采用混合方法分析

Result: 干预对会议效果的影响无统计学显著性；混合方法发现两组在自我报告的目标意识和行为上均有改善；后会议调查意外成为干预手段；揭示了支持工作场所反思的挑战，包括工作流程、协作规范以及会议准备的态度和行为

Conclusion: 支持目标反思具有潜力，但评估和支持工作场所会议反思面临挑战；为设计支持会议意向性的技术提供了启示，需要考虑工作流程整合和协作规范

Abstract: Ineffective meetings are pervasive. Thinking ahead explicitly about meeting goals may improve effectiveness, but current collaboration platforms lack integrated support. We tested a lightweight goal-reflection intervention in a preregistered field experiment in a global technology company (361 employees, 7196 meetings). Over two weeks, workers in the treatment group completed brief pre-meeting surveys in their collaboration platform, nudging attention to goals for upcoming meetings. To measure impact, both treatment and control groups completed post-meeting surveys about meeting effectiveness. While the intervention impact on meeting effectiveness was not statistically significant, mixed-methods findings revealed improvements in self-reported awareness and behaviour across both groups, with post-meeting surveys unintentionally functioning as an intervention. We highlight the promise of supporting goal reflection, while noting challenges of evaluating and supporting workplace reflection for meetings, including workflow and collaboration norms, and attitudes and behaviours around meeting preparation. We conclude with implications for designing technological support for meeting intentionality.

</details>


### [230] ["It's like a pet...but my pet doesn't collect data about me": Multi-person Households' Privacy Design Preferences for Household Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16975)
*Jennica Li,Shirley Zhang,Dakota Sullivan,Bengisu Cagiltay,Heather Kirkorian,Bilge Mutlu,Kassem Fawaz*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究通过参与式设计探索多用户家庭机器人隐私设计偏好，发现用户对机器人及制造商缺乏信任，提出数据控制权、可访问控制、个性化定制等设计建议


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家庭机器人普及带来隐私风险，现有研究缺乏多用户视角下的隐私设计偏好和缓解策略研究，需要填补这一空白

Method: 对15个家庭进行面对面参与式设计会议，探索他们如何基于隐私担忧和期望设计隐私感知的家庭机器人

Result: 参与者不信任机器人或制造商能尊重家庭成员的隐私数据或在多用户生态系统中保护个人数据，他们提出的设计强调数据控制权、可访问的控制和通知系统，以及可随时间定制以满足每个用户需求和偏好的能力

Conclusion: 研究结果为机器人制造商和开发者提供了可操作的设计建议，强调需要建立用户信任、提供数据控制权、设计可访问的隐私控制界面，并支持个性化定制以适应多用户家庭环境

Abstract: Household robots boasting mobility, more sophisticated sensors, and powerful processing models have become increasingly prevalent in the commercial market. However, these features may expose users to unwanted privacy risks, including unsolicited data collection and unauthorized data sharing. While security and privacy researchers thus far have explored people's privacy concerns around household robots, literature investigating people's preferred privacy designs and mitigation strategies is still limited. Additionally, the existing literature has not yet accounted for multi-user perspectives on privacy design and household robots. We aimed to fill this gap by conducting in-person participatory design sessions with 15 households to explore how they would design a privacy-aware household robot based on their concerns and expectations. We found that participants did not trust that robots, or their respective manufacturers, would respect the data privacy of household members or operate in a multi-user ecosystem without jeopardizing users' personal data. Based on these concerns, they generated designs that gave them authority over their data, contained accessible controls and notification systems, and could be customized and tailored to suit the needs and preferences of each user over time. We synthesize our findings into actionable design recommendations for robot manufacturers and developers.

</details>


### [231] [StoryLensEdu: Personalized Learning Report Generation through Narrative-Driven Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17067)
*Leixian Shen,Yan Luo,Rui Sheng,Yujia He,Haotian Li,Leni Yang,Huamin Qu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: StoryLensEdu：基于多智能体叙事的个性化学习反馈系统，通过故事化报告解决传统学习反馈的可解释性差、呈现单调等问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前个性化学习反馈系统（如文本报告、学习分析仪表板）存在可解释性差、呈现方式单调、解释性有限等问题，需要更直观、吸引人且交互性强的解决方案来支持自主学习

Method: 开发StoryLensEdu多智能体系统，包含三个智能体：数据分析师（基于学习目标结构提取数据洞察）、教师（确保教育相关性并提供可行建议）、故事讲述者（使用英雄之旅叙事框架组织洞察）。系统支持生成后交互式问答以提高可解释性和用户参与度

Result: 在真实高中进行的形成性研究和与电子学习团队合作迭代开发，用户评估显示StoryLensEdu能增强用户参与度并促进对学习过程的更深入理解

Conclusion: StoryLensEdu通过叙事驱动的多智能体方法成功解决了传统学习反馈系统的局限性，提供了更直观、吸引人且交互性强的个性化学习报告，有效支持自主学习过程

Abstract: Personalized feedback plays an important role in self-regulated learning (SRL), helping students track progress and refine their strategies. However, current common solutions, such as text-based reports or learning analytics dashboards, often suffer from poor interpretability, monotonous presentation, and limited explainability. To overcome these challenges, we present StoryLensEdu, a narrative-driven multi-agent system that automatically generates intuitive, engaging, and interactive learning reports. StoryLensEdu integrates three agents: a Data Analyst that extracts data insights based on a learning objective centered structure, a Teacher that ensures educational relevance and offers actionable suggestions, and a Storyteller that organizes these insights using the Heroes Journey narrative framework. StoryLensEdu supports post-generation interactive question answering to improve explainability and user engagement. We conducted a formative study in a real high school and iteratively developed StoryLensEdu in collaboration with an e-learning team to inform our design. Evaluation with real users shows that StoryLensEdu enhances engagement and promotes a deeper understanding of the learning process.

</details>


### [232] [Understanding Nature Engagement Experiences of Blind People](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17093)
*Mengjie Tang,Xinman Li,Juxiao Zhang,Franklin Mingzhe Li,Zhuying Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究调查盲人与视力正常者在自然关联性上的差异，探索盲人如何体验自然以及影响他们参与自然活动的因素，并为支持盲人自然参与的技术提供设计建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然对人类健康和福祉至关重要，但人们对盲人如何体验和与自然互动知之甚少。研究旨在填补这一知识空白，探索盲人如何超越视觉体验自然，以及影响他们参与自然活动的因素。

Method: 采用混合方法：1) 对20名盲人和20名视力正常参与者进行自然关联性问卷调查；2) 对16名盲人参与者进行深度访谈，分析他们与自然互动的实践、挑战、态度和价值观。

Result: 调查结果显示盲人参与者的自然关联性水平低于视力正常同龄人。访谈研究进一步揭示了：1) 当前自然参与的实践和挑战；2) 影响参与的态度和价值观；3) 对支持安全有意义参与的自然辅助技术的期望。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了盲人如何超越视觉体验自然，为支持包容性自然参与的技术奠定了基础。研究结果为未来支持盲人自然参与的技术设计提供了具体指导。

Abstract: Nature plays a crucial role in human health and well-being, but little is known about how blind people experience and relate to it. We conducted a survey of nature relatedness with blind (N=20) and sighted (N=20) participants, along with in-depth interviews with 16 blind participants, to examine how blind people engage with nature and the factors shaping this engagement. Our survey results revealed lower levels of nature relatedness among blind participants compared to sighted peers. Our interview study further highlighted: 1) current practices and challenges of nature engagement, 2) attitudes and values that shape engagement, and 3) expectations for assistive technologies that support safe and meaningful engagement. We also provide design implications to guide future technologies that support nature engagement for blind people. Overall, our findings illustrate how blind people experience nature beyond vision and lay a foundation for technologies that support inclusive nature engagement.

</details>


### [233] [NotebookRAG: Retrieving Multiple Notebooks to Augment the Generation of EDA Notebooks for Crowd-Wisdom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17215)
*Yi Shan,Yixuan He,Zekai Shao,Kai Xu,Siming Chen*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: NotebookRAG：一种基于检索增强生成的自动化EDA方法，通过利用现有分析笔记本中的知识来生成高质量、符合用户意图的探索性数据分析


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM方法在用户意图抽象时难以生成有效的分析计划、洞察和可视化，而大量现有分析笔记本包含丰富知识但未被充分利用。需要一种方法能有效利用这些知识来自动化EDA过程。

Method: 提出NotebookRAG方法：1）检索阶段将代码单元转换为上下文增强的可执行组件，提高检索质量并支持用新数据重新运行生成更新的可视化；2）生成阶段使用智能体利用增强的检索内容构建EDA计划、推导洞察并生成可视化。

Result: 24名参与者的用户研究证实该方法在生成高质量且符合用户意图的EDA笔记本方面具有优越性。

Conclusion: NotebookRAG通过有效检索和利用现有分析笔记本中的知识，显著提升了自动化EDA的质量和与用户意图的对齐度，为解决抽象意图下的EDA生成问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: High-quality exploratory data analysis (EDA) is essential in the data science pipeline, but remains highly dependent on analysts' expertise and effort. While recent LLM-based approaches partially reduce this burden, they struggle to generate effective analysis plans and appropriate insights and visualizations when user intent is abstract. Meanwhile, a vast collection of analysis notebooks produced across platforms and organizations contains rich analytical knowledge that can potentially guide automated EDA. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) provides a natural way to leverage such corpora, but general methods often treat notebooks as static documents and fail to fully exploit their potential knowledge for automating EDA. To address these limitations, we propose NotebookRAG, a method that takes user intent, datasets, and existing notebooks as input to retrieve, enhance, and reuse relevant notebook content for automated EDA generation. For retrieval, we transform code cells into context-enriched executable components, which improve retrieval quality and enable rerun with new data to generate updated visualizations and reliable insights. For generation, an agent leverages enhanced retrieval content to construct effective EDA plans, derive insights, and produce appropriate visualizations. Evidence from a user study with 24 participants confirms the superiority of our method in producing high-quality and intent-aligned EDA notebooks.

</details>


### [234] [PersonaMail: Learning and Adapting Personal Communication Preferences for Context-Aware Email Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17340)
*Rui Yao,Qiuyuan Ren,Felicia Fang-Yi Tan,Chen Yang,Xiaoyu Zhang,Shengdong Zhao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: PersonaMail是一个LLM辅助的邮件写作系统，通过结构化沟通因素探索、粒度编辑控制和成功策略自适应重用，解决了当前系统在捕捉微妙语气方面的不足，在效率和用户满意度方面优于标准LLM界面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM辅助写作系统在人际沟通中快速普及，但往往无法捕捉对沟通有效性至关重要的微妙语气。邮件写作尤其体现了这一挑战：有效信息需要与意图、关系和上下文进行精细对齐，而不仅仅是流畅性。通过形成性研究，发现了三个关键挑战：表达细微的沟通意图、在多个粒度级别进行修改，以及在消息间重用有效的语气策略。

Method: 开发了PersonaMail系统，通过三个核心机制解决上述挑战：1) 结构化沟通因素探索，帮助用户明确表达沟通意图；2) 粒度编辑控制，支持在不同抽象级别进行修改；3) 自适应重用成功策略，允许用户跨消息应用有效的语气策略。系统评估采用对比实验，将PersonaMail与标准LLM界面进行比较。

Result: 评估结果显示，PersonaMail在即时使用和重复使用中都表现出更高的效率，同时获得更高的用户满意度。系统在帮助用户创建更符合意图、关系和上下文的邮件方面优于标准LLM界面。

Conclusion: PersonaMail展示了AI辅助沟通系统的设计方向应优先考虑人际细微差别而非通用文本生成。研究为开发更有效的LLM辅助沟通工具提供了设计启示，强调了结构化意图表达、粒度控制和策略重用机制的重要性。

Abstract: LLM-assisted writing has seen rapid adoption in interpersonal communication, yet current systems often fail to capture the subtle tones essential for effectiveness. Email writing exemplifies this challenge: effective messages require careful alignment with intent, relationship, and context beyond mere fluency. Through formative studies, we identified three key challenges: articulating nuanced communicative intent, making modifications at multiple levels of granularity, and reusing effective tone strategies across messages. We developed PersonaMail, a system that addresses these gaps through structured communication factor exploration, granular editing controls, and adaptive reuse of successful strategies. Our evaluation compared PersonaMail against standard LLM interfaces, and showed improved efficiency in both immediate and repeated use, alongside higher user satisfaction. We contribute design implications for AI-assisted communication systems that prioritize interpersonal nuance over generic text generation.

</details>


### [235] [ShadAR: LLM-driven shader generation to transform visual perception in Augmented Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17481)
*Yanni Mei,Samuel Wendt,Florian Mueller,Jan Gugenheimer*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ShadAR：基于大语言模型的AR实时视觉感知转换系统，通过自然语言生成着色器代码，实现灵活的可视化效果变换


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AR系统需要开发者预先定义每种视觉效果，限制了灵活性。现有方法无法根据用户自然语言描述实时生成自定义视觉感知效果，特别是在辅助色盲人士等包容性应用场景中缺乏灵活性。

Method: 开发了ShadAR系统，采用大语言模型驱动的着色器生成流水线：1）用户通过自然语言表达视觉意图；2）LLM解析语言描述并生成相应的着色器代码；3）实时编译着色器代码；4）在AR头显视口中应用生成的着色器来修改视觉感知。

Result: 成功实现了基于自然语言的实时视觉感知转换，能够为包容性（如模拟色盲视觉）和创造性应用生成各种视觉效果。系统展示了LLM在生成可执行着色器代码方面的能力，实现了灵活、用户驱动的AR视觉体验定制。

Conclusion: ShadAR通过LLM驱动的着色器生成，突破了传统AR系统需要预定义视觉效果的局限，实现了灵活、实时的视觉感知转换，为AR的包容性和创造性应用开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Augmented Reality (AR) can simulate various visual perceptions, such as how individuals with colorblindness see the world. However, these simulations require developers to predefine each visual effect, limiting flexibility. We present ShadAR, an AR application enabling real-time transformation of visual perception through shader generation using large language models (LLMs). ShadAR allows users to express their visual intent via natural language, which is interpreted by an LLM to generate corresponding shader code. This shader is then compiled real-time to modify the AR headset viewport. We present our LLM-driven shader generation pipeline and demonstrate its ability to transform visual perception for inclusiveness and creativity.

</details>


### [236] [What Do LLMs Associate with Your Name? A Human-Centered Black-Box Audit of Personal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17483)
*Dimitri Staufer,Kirsten Morehouse*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文研究了大型语言模型(LLMs)如何处理个人数据(PD)，开发了LMP2隐私审计工具，并通过实证研究揭示了LLMs能够准确生成用户个人信息，引发了关于个人数据定义和隐私权利的讨论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在预训练和用户交互中暴露于个人数据，现有研究表明个人数据可能重新浮现，但用户缺乏对模型如何将特定信息与其身份关联的洞察。需要开发工具来审计模型中的个人数据关联，并了解用户对此的认知和反应。

Method: 1) 审计了8个LLM模型(3个开源，5个API包括GPT-4o)；2) 开发LMP2(Language Model Privacy Probe)工具，通过两个形成性研究(N=20)进行优化；3) 对欧盟居民进行两项研究：研究一(N1=155)了解用户对LLM生成个人数据的直觉，研究二(N2=303)观察用户对工具输出的反应。

Result: 1) 模型能够自信地为知名人士生成多个类别的个人数据；2) 对于普通用户，GPT-4o能够以60%或更高的准确率生成11个特征(如性别、发色、语言等)；3) 72%的参与者希望控制模型生成的与其姓名相关的关联，这引发了关于什么构成个人数据以及数据隐私权利是否应扩展到LLMs的讨论。

Conclusion: LLMs能够准确生成用户的个人信息，这引发了重要的隐私问题。用户对模型生成的关联缺乏控制权，需要重新思考个人数据的定义范围，并考虑将数据隐私权利扩展到大型语言模型领域。LMP2工具为隐私审计提供了有价值的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs), and conversational agents based on them, are exposed to personal data (PD) during pre-training and during user interactions. Prior work shows that PD can resurface, yet users lack insight into how strongly models associate specific information to their identity. We audit PD across eight LLMs (3 open-source; 5 API-based, including GPT-4o), introduce LMP2 (Language Model Privacy Probe), a human-centered, privacy-preserving audit tool refined through two formative studies (N=20), and run two studies with EU residents to capture (i) intuitions about LLM-generated PD (N1=155) and (ii) reactions to tool output (N2=303). We show empirically that models confidently generate multiple PD categories for well-known individuals. For everyday users, GPT-4o generates 11 features with 60% or more accuracy (e.g., gender, hair color, languages). Finally, 72% of participants sought control over model-generated associations with their name, raising questions about what counts as PD and whether data privacy rights should extend to LLMs.

</details>


### [237] [The Effectiveness of a Virtual Reality-Based Training Program for Improving Body Awareness in Children with Attention Deficit and Hyperactivity Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17649)
*Aya Abdelnaem El-Basha,Ebtsam ELSayed Mahmoud ELSayes,Ahmad Al-Kabbany*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: VR训练能有效改善ADHD儿童的身体意识，效果可持续


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索虚拟现实训练对改善注意力缺陷多动障碍儿童身体意识的有效性，为早期干预提供新方法

Method: 采用准实验设计，将10名4-7岁ADHD儿童分为实验组和对照组，实验组接受为期3个月共36次的结构化VR干预，使用斯坦福-比奈智力量表、康纳斯ADHD测试和研究者自编身体意识量表进行评估

Result: 实验组前后测得分存在统计学显著差异，VR训练在空间意识、身体部位识别和运动表达方面效果显著；干预后一个月随访评估显示效果持续，无显著差异

Conclusion: 沉浸式VR环境为ADHD儿童早期心理运动缺陷干预提供了安全、吸引人且有效的治疗媒介，效果具有持续性

Abstract: This study investigates the effectiveness of a Virtual Reality (VR)-based training program in improving body awareness among children with Attention Deficit Hyperactivity Disorder (ADHD). Utilizing a quasi-experimental design, the research sample consisted of 10 children aged 4 to 7 years, with IQ scores ranging from 90 to 110. Participants were divided into an experimental group and a control group, with the experimental group receiving a structured VR intervention over three months, totaling 36 sessions. Assessment tools included the Stanford-Binet Intelligence Scale (5th Edition), the Conners Test for ADHD, and a researcher-prepared Body Awareness Scale.
  The results indicated statistically significant differences between pre-test and post-test scores for the experimental group, demonstrating the program's efficacy in enhancing spatial awareness, body part identification, and motor expressions. Furthermore, follow-up assessments conducted one month after the intervention revealed no significant differences from the post-test results, confirming the sustainability and continuity of the program's effects over time. The findings suggest that immersive VR environments provide a safe, engaging, and effective therapeutic medium for addressing psychomotor deficits in early childhood ADHD.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [238] [Beyond Procedure: Substantive Fairness in Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16794)
*Pengqi Liu,Zijun Yu,Mouloud Belbahri,Arthur Charpentier,Masoud Asgharian,Jesse C. Cresswell*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 该论文研究共形预测与公平性的交互，提出超越程序公平性分析下游决策结果公平性，通过理论分析和LLM评估方法发现标签聚类共形预测能提升实质性公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测提供分布无关的不确定性量化，但其与下游决策公平性的交互关系尚未充分探索。现有研究多关注共形预测作为独立操作的程序公平性，而缺乏对整个决策流程中实质性公平性的分析。

Method: 1) 理论推导预测集大小差异的上界，分解为可解释组件，阐明标签聚类共形预测如何控制方法驱动的公平性问题；2) 引入LLM-in-the-loop评估器，近似人类对多模态实质性公平性的评估；3) 通过实验比较不同共形预测变体。

Result: 1) 标签聚类共形预测变体始终提供更优的实质性公平性；2) 预测集大小均等化（而非覆盖率均等化）与改善的实质性公平性强相关；3) LLM评估器能够有效近似人类对公平性的判断。

Conclusion: 标签聚类共形预测能有效提升下游决策的实质性公平性，预测集大小均等化是设计公平共形预测系统的关键指标，为实践者提供了构建更公平系统的指导。

Abstract: Conformal prediction (CP) offers distribution-free uncertainty quantification for machine learning models, yet its interplay with fairness in downstream decision-making remains underexplored. Moving beyond CP as a standalone operation (procedural fairness), we analyze the holistic decision-making pipeline to evaluate substantive fairness-the equity of downstream outcomes. Theoretically, we derive an upper bound that decomposes prediction-set size disparity into interpretable components, clarifying how label-clustered CP helps control method-driven contributions to unfairness. To facilitate scalable empirical analysis, we introduce an LLM-in-the-loop evaluator that approximates human assessment of substantive fairness across diverse modalities. Our experiments reveal that label-clustered CP variants consistently deliver superior substantive fairness. Finally, we empirically show that equalized set sizes, rather than coverage, strongly correlate with improved substantive fairness, enabling practitioners to design more fair CP systems. Our code is available at https://github.com/layer6ai-labs/llm-in-the-loop-conformal-fairness.

</details>


### [239] [Poisson-MNL Bandit: Nearly Optimal Dynamic Joint Assortment and Pricing with Decision-Dependent Customer Arrivals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.16923)
*Junhui Cai,Ran Chen,Qitao Huang,Linda Zhao,Wu Zhu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 论文提出Poisson-MNL模型，将上下文MNL选择模型与依赖品类和价格的泊松到达模型结合，开发了基于UCB的PMNL算法，实现了√(TlogT)的遗憾界，并通过仿真验证了考虑到达率依赖性的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MNL模型假设顾客到达率固定，忽略了品类和定价对顾客到达数量的影响，可能导致次优决策。在实际零售场景中，品类和价格不仅影响顾客购买选择，也影响当期顾客到达数量。

Method: 提出Poisson-MNL模型，结合上下文MNL选择模型和依赖品类价格的泊松到达模型。基于该模型开发PMNL算法，采用上置信界(UCB)方法，在定期会计/运营间隔更新品类和定价决策。

Result: 证明了PMNL算法的非渐进遗憾界为√(TlogT)，并给出了匹配的下界（最多差logT因子）。仿真研究表明，考虑到达率依赖性的PMNL算法显著优于假设固定到达率的方法。

Conclusion: 考虑品类和定价对顾客到达率的影响至关重要。Poisson-MNL模型和PMNL算法能够有效学习顾客选择和到达模型，提供优于传统方法的联合品类定价决策。

Abstract: We study dynamic joint assortment and pricing where a seller updates decisions at regular accounting/operating intervals to maximize the cumulative per-period revenue over a horizon $T$. In many settings, assortment and prices affect not only what an arriving customer buys but also how many customers arrive within the period, whereas classical multinomial logit (MNL) models assume arrivals as fixed, potentially leading to suboptimal decisions. We propose a Poisson-MNL model that couples a contextual MNL choice model with a Poisson arrival model whose rate depends on the offered assortment and prices. Building on this model, we develop an efficient algorithm PMNL based on the idea of upper confidence bound (UCB). We establish its (near) optimality by proving a non-asymptotic regret bound of order $\sqrt{T\log{T}}$ and a matching lower bound (up to $\log T$). Simulation studies underscore the importance of accounting for the dependency of arrival rates on assortment and pricing: PMNL effectively learns customer choice and arrival models and provides joint assortment-pricing decisions that outperform others that assume fixed arrival rates.

</details>


### [240] [Anti-causal domain generalization: Leveraging unlabeled data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17187)
*Sorawit Saengkyongam,Juan L. Gamella,Andrew C. Miller,Jonas Peters,Nicolai Meinshausen,Christina Heinze-Deml*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出两种无需标签数据的域泛化方法，通过正则化模型对跨环境协变量扰动的敏感性，在反因果设置下实现分布鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有域泛化方法通常需要多个训练环境的标记数据，这在标记数据稀缺时限制了应用。本文研究反因果设置下的域泛化问题，其中结果变量导致观察到的协变量。在这种结构下，影响协变量的环境扰动不会传播到结果变量，这为通过正则化模型对这些扰动的敏感性提供了理论基础。

Method: 提出两种方法：1) 惩罚模型对跨环境协变量均值变化的敏感性；2) 惩罚模型对跨环境协变量协方差变化的敏感性。这些方法的关键优势在于估计扰动方向不需要标签，因此可以利用多个环境的未标记数据。方法基于反因果结构，其中环境扰动影响协变量但不影响结果变量。

Result: 证明了这些方法在特定环境类别下具有最坏情况最优性保证。在受控物理系统和生理信号数据集上展示了方法的实证性能，验证了其在域泛化任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 在反因果设置下，通过正则化模型对跨环境协变量扰动的敏感性，可以实现无需标签数据的域泛化。该方法利用未标记数据估计扰动方向，具有理论保证并在实际应用中表现出良好性能，为解决标记数据稀缺时的域泛化问题提供了新途径。

Abstract: The problem of domain generalization concerns learning predictive models that are robust to distribution shifts when deployed in new, previously unseen environments. Existing methods typically require labeled data from multiple training environments, limiting their applicability when labeled data are scarce. In this work, we study domain generalization in an anti-causal setting, where the outcome causes the observed covariates. Under this structure, environment perturbations that affect the covariates do not propagate to the outcome, which motivates regularizing the model's sensitivity to these perturbations. Crucially, estimating these perturbation directions does not require labels, enabling us to leverage unlabeled data from multiple environments. We propose two methods that penalize the model's sensitivity to variations in the mean and covariance of the covariates across environments, respectively, and prove that these methods have worst-case optimality guarantees under certain classes of environments. Finally, we demonstrate the empirical performance of our approach on a controlled physical system and a physiological signal dataset.

</details>


### [241] [genriesz: A Python Package for Automatic Debiased Machine Learning with Generalized Riesz Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17543)
*Masahiro Kato*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: genriesz是一个Python包，通过Riesz表示定理和去偏机器学习自动估计因果和结构参数，提供广义Riesz回归框架和自动回归器平衡功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一个开源工具，自动化因果和结构参数的高效估计，统一多种估计方法（如协变量平衡、最近邻匹配、校准估计等），简化研究人员的工作流程。

Method: 基于Riesz表示定理和去偏机器学习，实现广义Riesz回归框架，通过最小化经验Bregman散度估计Riesz表示子。核心设计原则是自动回归器平衡（ARB），根据Bregman生成器和表示子模型类自动构建兼容的链接函数。

Result: 开发了genriesz Python包，提供模块化接口用于指定目标线性泛函、表示子模型和Bregman生成器，返回回归调整、Riesz加权、增强Riesz加权和TMLE风格估计器，支持交叉拟合、置信区间和p值计算。

Conclusion: genriesz提供了一个统一、自动化的框架来估计因果和结构参数，将多种现有方法纳入广义Riesz回归框架，通过自动回归器平衡确保最优性条件，简化了因果推断的实践应用。

Abstract: Efficient estimation of causal and structural parameters can be automated using the Riesz representation theorem and debiased machine learning (DML). We present genriesz, an open-source Python package that implements automatic DML and generalized Riesz regression, a unified framework for estimating Riesz representers by minimizing empirical Bregman divergences. This framework includes covariate balancing, nearest-neighbor matching, calibrated estimation, and density ratio estimation as special cases. A key design principle of the package is automatic regressor balancing (ARB): given a Bregman generator $g$ and a representer model class, genriesz} automatically constructs a compatible link function so that the generalized Riesz regression estimator satisfies balancing (moment-matching) optimality conditions in a user-chosen basis. The package provides a modulr interface for specifying (i) the target linear functional via a black-box evaluation oracle, (ii) the representer model via basis functions (polynomial, RKHS approximations, random forest leaf encodings, neural embeddings, and a nearest-neighbor catchment basis), and (iii) the Bregman generator, with optional user-supplied derivatives. It returns regression adjustment (RA), Riesz weighting (RW), augmented Riesz weighting (ARW), and TMLE-style estimators with cross-fitting, confidence intervals, and $p$-values. We highlight representative workflows for estimation problems such as the average treatment effect (ATE), ATE on treated (ATT), and average marginal effect estimation. The Python package is available at https://github.com/MasaKat0/genriesz and on PyPI.

</details>


### [242] [SOLVAR: Fast covariance-based heterogeneity analysis with pose refinement for cryo-EM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2602.17603)
*Roey Yadgar,Roy R. Lederman,Yoel Shkolnisky*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: SOLVAR是一种基于随机优化的低秩变异性分析方法，用于冷冻电镜中连续异质性的高效协方差矩阵估计和构象分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冷冻电镜中连续异质性分析的关键挑战是高效估计协方差矩阵，传统方法计算成本高昂，且大多数方法无法同时优化粒子图像姿态。

Method: 利用协方差矩阵的低秩假设，将问题转化为可快速求解的优化问题，同时实现了粒子图像姿态的联合优化，这是大多数异质性分析方法所不具备的能力。

Result: 在合成和实验数据集上的数值实验表明，该算法能准确捕捉主要变异性成分，同时保持计算效率，在最近的异质性基准测试中达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: SOLVAR提供了一种高效、准确的连续异质性分析方法，通过低秩假设和随机优化解决了协方差矩阵估计的计算难题，并实现了姿态优化的独特功能。

Abstract: Cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM) has emerged as a powerful technique for resolving the three-dimensional structures of macromolecules. A key challenge in cryo-EM is characterizing continuous heterogeneity, where molecules adopt a continuum of conformational states. Covariance-based methods offer a principled approach to modeling structural variability. However, estimating the covariance matrix efficiently remains a challenging computational task. In this paper, we present SOLVAR (Stochastic Optimization for Low-rank Variability Analysis), which leverages a low-rank assumption on the covariance matrix to provide a tractable estimator for its principal components, despite the apparently prohibitive large size of the covariance matrix. Under this low-rank assumption, our estimator can be formulated as an optimization problem that can be solved quickly and accurately. Moreover, our framework enables refinement of the poses of the input particle images, a capability absent from most heterogeneity-analysis methods, and all covariance-based methods. Numerical experiments on both synthetic and experimental datasets demonstrate that the algorithm accurately captures dominant components of variability while maintaining computational efficiency. SOLVAR achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple datasets in a recent heterogeneity benchmark. The code of the algorithm is freely available at https://github.com/RoeyYadgar/SOLVAR.

</details>
